    if (false === dhd.Responsive.isSmartphone()) {
        // Binding ab sm (für Tablet Wechsel von portrait auf landscape)
        dhd.prep('dhd.header');
        dhd.header.primaryNavbars = document.querySelectorAll('.primary-navbar');
        window.addEventListener('scroll', function() {
            if (dhd.Responsive.isStationary()) {
                // Invertierung ab md
                dhd.header.primaryNavbars.forEach(primaryNavbar => {
                    var primaryNavbarClassList = primaryNavbar.classList;
                    if (primaryNavbar.offset().top === 0) {
                        primaryNavbarClassList.add('inverted');
                    } else if (primaryNavbarClassList.contains('inverted')) {
                        primaryNavbarClassList.remove('inverted');
                    }
                });
            }
        });
    }
</script>

            } catch (e) {
            }
        }
    };

        currentWidth = $(document).width();

        breakPoint = '';
        if (currentWidth < 720) {
            breakPoint = 'xs';
        } else if (currentWidth < 1200) {
            breakPoint = 'sm';

            if (true === dhd.header.isLoggedIn()) {
                ga('set', 'userId', dhd.header.getAnalyticsUserId());

            } else {
                ga('set', 'dimension3', 'n');
            }

            // Nutzergruppe bestimmen (ga Dimension 18)
            var targetGroup = dhd.header.getTargetGroupId();

            if (targetGroup === 2) {
                ga('set', 'dimension18', 'seller');
            } else {
                ga('set', 'dimension18', 'potentialBuyer');

    });

<script type="text/javascript">

	
    dhd.prep('bookmark');
    // Wenn die Anzeige auf dem Merkzettel steht, dann markieren
    dhd.bookmark.processBookmarkedClassifieds(true);

	
    dhd.prep('profile');
    dhd.prep('reaction');

    /**
     *
     * @type {ReactionBarController[]}
     */
    dhd.profile.reactionBarControllers = [];

    dhd.profile.topPostsController = new dhd.profile.TopPostsController('index-content-recent-posts');

    window.addEventListener('dhd.topPostsRendered', function(event) {

        Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.getElementsByClassName('reaction-bar'), function(element) {
            var postId = parseInt(element.closest('.js-recent-post-box').dataset.postId);
            var post   = event.detail.posts[postId];

            // Bei unserem eigenen Teaser brauchen wir keine Reaktionen ;)
            if ('undefined' === typeof post.isTeaser || false === post.isTeaser) {
                dhd.profile.reactionBarControllers.push(new dhd.reaction.ReactionBarController(element, post.reactions, postId));
            }
        });

        dhd.header.loginChecked = dhd.header.loginChecked || [];
        dhd.header.loginChecked.push(function() {
            if (!dhd.profile.reactionBarControllers.length) return;

            for (var reactionBarControllerIndex in dhd.profile.reactionBarControllers) {
                if (!dhd.profile.reactionBarControllers.hasOwnProperty(reactionBarControllerIndex)) continue;
                dhd.profile.reactionBarControllers[reactionBarControllerIndex].applyCustomerId(dhd.header.getUserId());
            }
        });
    });

	
    jQuery(function() {
        jQuery.scrollDepth({
            minHeight: 600,
            gtmOverride: true,
            userTiming: false,
            pixelDepth: false
        });
    });

	
    dhd.prep('dhd.header');
    dhd.header.loginChecked = dhd.header.loginChecked || [];

    dhd.header.loginChecked.push(function () {

        if (dhd.header.isLoggedIn()) {

            window.intercomSettings = {
                

                
                    is_app_user: false,
                

                app_id: "owx3uepa",
                email: dhd.header.getUserMail(),

                is_seller: dhd.header.isSeller(),
                is_potential_buyer: dhd.header.isPotentialBuyer(),
                user_id: dhd.header.getUserId(),
                user_hash: dhd.header.getIcomHash(),
                user_name: dhd.header.getUserName(),
                name: dhd.header.getName(),
                created_at: dhd.header.getRegistrationDate(),
                is_premium_member: dhd.header.isPremiumMember()
            };

            

            if (typeof dhd.intercom !== "undefined") {

                if (typeof dhd.intercom.events !== "undefined") {
                    dhd.whenAvailable('Intercom', function () {
                        dhd.intercom.events.forEach(function(element) {
                            Intercom('trackEvent', element.name, element.metadata);
                        });
                    });
                }

                if (typeof dhd.intercom.lastPurchaseTotalAmount !== "undefined") {
                    window.intercomSettings.last_purchase_total_amount = dhd.intercom.lastPurchaseTotalAmount;
                }
                if (typeof dhd.intercom.lastCartTotalAmount !== "undefined") {
                    window.intercomSettings.last_cart_total_amount = dhd.intercom.lastCartTotalAmount;
                }
                if (typeof dhd.intercom.splittest !== "undefined") {
                    window.intercomSettings.testgroup = dhd.intercom.splittest;
                }
            }

        } else {

            window.intercomSettings = {
                

                
                is_app_user: false,
                

                app_id: "owx3uepa"
            };

            

            

        }

        (function(){var w=window;var ic=w.Intercom;if(typeof ic==="function"){ic('reattach_activator');ic('update',w.intercomSettings);}else{var d=document;var i=function(){i.c(arguments);};i.q=[];i.c=function(args){i.q.push(args);};w.Intercom=i;var l=function(){var s=d.createElement('script');s.type='text/javascript';s.async=true;s.src='https://widget.intercom.io/widget/owx3uepa';var x=d.getElementsByTagName('script')[0];x.parentNode.insertBefore(s,x);};if(w.attachEvent){w.attachEvent('onload',l);}else{w.addEventListener('load',l,false);}}})();
    });

</script>
